[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Design a module named fp_mul_e5f10 that multiplies two floating-point numbers. Format: 16-bit (1 sign + 5 exponent + 10 fraction). Inputs: a and b (16-bit each). Output: y (16-bit). Subnormal numbers must be supported.

Required behaviour, bit-exact: subnormal inputs are used at their true value; results are rounded to nearest, ties to even, including results in the subnormal range; a NaN input and inf x 0 give the canonical quiet NaN 16'h7E00; inf x finite gives inf with the product's sign; an overflowing product gives inf; a zero product gives a signed zero. The testbench enumerates about 18.9 million operand pairs against a reference implementation.

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` in your workspace, a plain readable implementation (unpack, significand multiply, normalise, round, pack) that is correct. Optimize from there.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

[Step 1/60]
// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier (1 sign, 5 exponent, 10 fraction bits) with subnormal support.

module fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);
  input  [15:0] a;
  input  [15:0] b;
  output [15:0] y;

  localparam BIAS         = 15;
  localparam MAX_E        = 31;   // inf / NaN
  localparam MAX_FINITE_E = 30;
  localparam PROD_W       = 22;   // 11 x 11 bit significand product

  // 1. unpack
  wire       s_a = a[15];
  wire       s_b = b[15];
  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];
  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];
  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];
  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];

  // 2. classify
  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);
  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);
  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);
  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);
  wire zero_a   = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);
  wire zero_b   = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);
  wire nan_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);
  wire nan_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);
  wire inf_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);
  wire inf_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);

  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);
  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;
  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;
  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;

  // 3. effective significands and exponents (subnormal: no hidden one, exponent 1)
  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};
  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};
  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;
  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;

  // 4. significand product, its leading-zero count (0 for a zero product), below[k] = |prod[k:0]
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;

  reg [4:0] lz;
  integer   i;
  always @* begin
    lz = 5'd0;
    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)
      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];
  end

  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;
  genvar k;
  generate
    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below
      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];
    end
  endgenerate

  function bit_at;   // v[idx], 0 outside the vector
    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;
    input integer      idx;
    begin
      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;
    end
  endfunction

  // 5. normalise and round a normal result: leading one onto bit 10
  wire              need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);
  wire [4:0]        sr         = 5'd11 - lz;   // right shift (wide product)
  wire [4:0]        sl         = lz - 5'd11;   // left shift (narrow product)
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);
  wire [10:0]       mant_pre   = shifted[10:0];

  wire guard    = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;   // first bit shifted out
  wire sticky   = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;   // any bit below the guard
  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);              // round to nearest, ties to even

  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};
  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];   // rounded up to a new leading one
  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];
  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];

  // 6. exponent of a normal result
  wire [7:0] exp_sum     = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};
  wire [7:0] lhs         = exp_sum + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};
  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};
  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};
  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);   // subnormal (or zero) result
  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);    // past the largest finite number
  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;
  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];

  // 7. subnormal result: shift the raw product onto the subnormal grid, round once
  integer           total_shift;
  reg [PROD_W-1:0]  sub_shifted;
  always @* begin
    total_shift = (BIAS + 10 + 1) - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});
    sub_shifted = (total_shift >= 0 && total_shift < PROD_W) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};
  end
  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];
  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod,  total_shift - 1);
  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);
  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);
  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};
  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];   // rounded up to the smallest normal
  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];
  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};

  // 8. pack
  wire is_nan      = nan_in;
  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));
  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;
  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);
  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);

  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;
  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;
  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;

  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};
endmodule


[ASSISTANT]
  -> read_file(filename='tb.sv', limit=80)

[TOOL RESULT]
[Step 2/60]
`timescale 1ns/10ps
// exhaustive-directed tb (fpmul_run)
// Golden-referenced directed testbench: compares the DUT against the embedded
// golden reference (fp_mul_e5f10_ref, generated from context/starting_point.py)
// over ~18.9M enumerated vectors — no stimuli file. Structure:
//   1. the 3 known subnormal-boundary regression pairs (+ golden self-checks)
//   2. every subnormal/zero a (2^11 sign+mantissa patterns) x 4096 structured b
//   3. 4096 structured a x every subnormal/zero b
//   4. 2^21 LFSR-driven broad pairs
// Structured operands: both signs x all 32 exponents x 64 mantissas
// (8 corners + 56 LFSR-derived). The retired file-driven testbench is kept at
// tb_vectors.sv (+ vectors.dat) for cross-checking; it is not compiled.
module tb;
  logic [15:0] a, b, y, y_ref;
  int errors;
  longint total;

  fp_mul_e5f10 dut (.a(a), .b(b), .y(y));
  fp_mul_e5f10_ref refm (.a(a), .b(b), .y(y_ref));

  function automatic [15:0] lfsr_next(input [15:0] s);
    lfsr_next = (s >> 1) ^ (s[0] ? 16'hB400 : 16'h0000);
  endfunction

  logic [15:0] structured [0:4095];
  localparam logic [9:0] MANT_CORNERS [0:7] =
      '{10'h000, 10'h001, 10'h002, 10'h003, 10'h3FF, 10'h3FE, 10'h2AA, 10'h155};

  task automatic check();
    #1;
    total = total + 1;
    if (y !== y_ref) begin
      if (errors < 10)
        $display("TB_ERROR a=%0d b=%0d expected_y=%0d actual_y=%0d", a, b, y_ref, y);
      errors = errors + 1;
    end
  endtask

  logic [15:0] lfsr, ra, rb;
  logic [9:0] mant;
  int idx;

  initial begin
    errors = 0; total = 0;

    lfsr = 16'hACE1;
    idx = 0;
    for (int s = 0; s < 2; s++)
      for (int e = 0; e < 32; e++)
        for (int m = 0; m < 64; m++) begin
          if (m < 8) mant = MANT_CORNERS[m];
          else begin lfsr = lfsr_next(lfsr); mant = lfsr[9:0]; end
          structured[idx] = {s[0], e[4:0], mant};
          idx = idx + 1;
        end

    a = 16'h0003; b = 16'h6801; check();

[ASSISTANT]
The starting point looks solid. Let me create the design file from it and run the first evaluation.
  -> create_file(filename='design.sv', content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier (1 sign, 5 exponent, 10 fraction bits) with subnormal support.\n\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  localparam BIAS         = 15;\n  localparam MAX_E        = 31;   // inf / NaN\n  localparam MAX_FINITE_E = 30;\n  localparam PROD_W       = 22;   // 11 x 11 bit significand product\n\n  // 1. unpack\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  // 2. classify\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);\n  wire zero_a   = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_b   = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);\n  wire nan_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);\n  wire nan_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);\n  wire inf_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire inf_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  // 3. effective significands and exponents (subnormal: no hidden one, exponent 1)\n  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  // 4. significand product, its leading-zero count (0 for a zero product), below[k] = |prod[k:0]\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  reg [4:0] lz;\n  integer   i;\n  always @* begin\n    lz = 5'd0;\n    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)\n      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];\n  end\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  function bit_at;   // v[idx], 0 outside the vector\n    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;\n    input integer      idx;\n    begin\n      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  // 5. normalise and round a normal result: leading one onto bit 10\n  wire              need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);\n  wire [4:0]        sr         = 5'd11 - lz;   // right shift (wide product)\n  wire [4:0]        sl         = lz - 5'd11;   // left shift (narrow product)\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);\n  wire [10:0]       mant_pre   = shifted[10:0];\n\n  wire guard    = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;   // first bit shifted out\n  wire sticky   = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;   // any bit below the guard\n  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);              // round to nearest, ties to even\n\n  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};\n  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];   // rounded up to a new leading one\n  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];\n\n  // 6. exponent of a normal result\n  wire [7:0] exp_sum     = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};\n  wire [7:0] lhs         = exp_sum + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};\n  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);   // subnormal (or zero) result\n  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);    // past the largest finite number\n  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];\n\n  // 7. subnormal result: shift the raw product onto the subnormal grid, round once\n  integer           total_shift;\n  reg [PROD_W-1:0]  sub_shifted;\n  always @* begin\n    total_shift = (BIAS + 10 + 1) - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});\n    sub_shifted = (total_shift >= 0 && total_shift < PROD_W) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n  end\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];\n  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod,  total_shift - 1);\n  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);\n  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];   // rounded up to the smallest normal\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];\n  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};\n\n  // 8. pack\n  wire is_nan      = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;\n  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);\n  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n")

The baseline passes at cost 145429 (area 108.7, delay 1338). Now let me analyze the optimization opportunity. The starting point computes **two parallel rounding paths** (normal + subnormal), each with its own shifter, rounding adder, and exponent logic, then muxes them at pack. I'll rewrite with a **unified datapath**: single shift amount S, single shifter, single rounding adder, shared exponent math (signed d = exp_sum − lz, E_raw = d−14, sub ⟺ d≤14, S = sub ? 26−exp_sum : max(11−lz,0)).

[ASSISTANT]
v2 passes: **140525** (area 95.3, delay 1474). Area dropped 12%, but delay rose. Since delay dominates the product, let me attack the critical path. Key insight: `S = max(11−lz, 26−exp_sum)` clamped at 0 — this removes the `d`/`sub` subtract-compare from the shifter's control path (the max picks the right shift automatically in both regimes):
  -> edit_file(filename='design_v2.sv', new_string="  // 5. shift amount: align rounded result onto target grid\n  //    normal: leading one -> bit 10 ; subnormal: fraction grid (g = prod >> (26-exp_sum))\n  //    unified: S = max(11 - lz, 26 - exp_sum, 0)  (the max selects the right regime)\n  wire signed [6:0] s_n  = 7'sd11 - $signed({2'b00, lz});\n  wire signed [6:0] s_s  = 7'sd26 - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});\n  wire signed [6:0] s_m  = (s_n > s_s) ? s_n : s_s;\n  wire [4:0]        S    = s_m[6] ? 5'd0 : s_m[4:0];", old_string="  // 5. shift amount: align rounded result onto target grid\n  //    normal: leading one -> bit 10 ; subnormal: fraction grid (g = prod >> (26-exp_sum))\n  wire [4:0] s_norm = (lz <= 5'd11) ? (5'd11 - lz) : 5'd0;\n  wire [5:0] s_sub6 = 6'd26 - exp_sum;          // >= 0 for all reachable subnormal cases\n  wire [4:0] S      = sub ? s_sub6[4:0] : s_norm;")

[ASSISTANT]
Good data: higher target_delay hurts (132872). The critical insight from the path dump: the **exponent path runs through the shifter + incrementer** (~7 extra levels). The rounding carry-out can be computed **directly from the product** without the shifter: `carry_n = prod[S-1] & (bits S..S+10 all ones)` using a prefix-OR of the inverted product. Let me build v3 with a carry bypass + balanced LZC trees:
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v3: unified datapath + carry bypass.\n// Key changes vs v2:\n//  - balanced (log-depth) leading-zero count trees\n//  - rounding carry-out computed directly from prod via prefix-OR arrays\n//    (no shifter / incrementer on the exponent critical path)\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // 1. unpack + classify\n  wire        s_a = a[15];\n  wire        s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0]  e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0]  f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = ~|e_a;\n  wire e_b_zero = ~|e_b;\n  wire e_a_ones =  &e_a;\n  wire e_b_ones =  &e_b;\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & ~|f_a;\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & ~|f_b;\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones &  |f_a;\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones &  |f_b;\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & ~|f_a;\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & ~|f_b;\n\n  wire nan_in = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire s_y    = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  // 2. effective significands / exponents (subnormals: no hidden one, eff exp 1)\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [5:0]  exp_sum = {1'b0, (e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a)} + {1'b0, (e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b)};\n\n  // 3. significand product (22 bits) and balanced leading-zero count\n  wire [21:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  wire g_hi = |prod[21:16];\n  wire g_md = |prod[15:8];\n\n  wire h01 = |prod[21:20];\n  wire h23 = |prod[19:18];\n  wire [2:0] czh0 = prod[21] ? 3'd0 : 3'd1;\n  wire [2:0] czh1 = prod[19] ? 3'd2 : 3'd3;\n  wire [2:0] czh2 = prod[17] ? 3'd4 : 3'd5;\n  wire [2:0] clz_hi = h01 ? czh0 : h23 ? czh1 : czh2;\n\n  wire m01 = |prod[15:12];\n  wire [3:0] czm0 = prod[15] ? 4'd0 : prod[14] ? 4'd1 : prod[13] ? 4'd2 : 4'd3;\n  wire [3:0] czm1 = prod[11] ? 4'd4 : prod[10] ? 4'd5 : prod[9] ? 4'd6 : 4'd7;\n  wire [3:0] clz_md = m01 ? czm0 : czm1;\n\n  wire l01 = |prod[7:4];\n  wire [3:0] czl0 = prod[7] ? 4'd0 : prod[6] ? 4'd1 : prod[5] ? 4'd2 : 4'd3;\n  wire [3:0] czl1 = prod[3] ? 4'd4 : prod[2] ? 4'd5 : prod[1] ? 4'd6 : prod[0] ? 4'd7 : 4'd8;\n  wire [3:0] clz_lo = l01 ? czl0 : czl1;\n\n  wire [4:0] lz = g_hi ? {2'b00, clz_hi} :\n                  g_md ? (5'd6 + {1'b0, clz_md}) :\n                         (5'd14 - {1'b0, clz_lo});\n\n  // 4. prefix reductions: below[k] = |prod[k:0] , bn[k] = |~prod[k:0]\n  wire [21:0] below;\n  wire [21:0] bn;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 22; k = k + 1) begin : g_red\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n      assign bn[k]    = |(~prod[k:0]);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  // 5. exponent bookkeeping (signed):\n  //    E_raw = exp_sum + p - 35 = d - 14,  d = exp_sum - lz\n  //    subnormal result  <=>  E_raw <= 0  <=>  d <= 14\n  wire signed [6:0] d    = $signed({1'b0, exp_sum}) - $signed({2'b0, lz});\n  wire              sub  = (d <= 7'sd14);\n\n  // 6. shift amount: S = max(11 - lz, 26 - exp_sum, 0)\n  wire signed [6:0] s_n  = 7'sd11 - $signed({2'b00, lz});\n  wire signed [6:0] s_s  = 7'sd26 - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});\n  wire signed [6:0] s_m  = (s_n > s_s) ? s_n : s_s;\n  wire [4:0]        S    = s_m[6] ? 5'd0 : s_m[4:0];\n\n  // 7. shift + round (single rounding stage, ties-to-even)\n  wire [21:0] shifted   = prod >> S;\n  wire [10:0] mant_pre  = shifted[10:0];\n\n  wire [63:0] prod_e = {42'd0, prod};\n  wire [63:0] bn_e   = {42'd0, bn};\n  wire [31:0] below_x = {10'd0, below};\n  wire [4:0]  idx_g   = S - 5'd1;\n  wire [5:0]  idx_w9  = {1'b0, S} + 6'd9;\n  wire [5:0]  idx_w10 = {1'b0, S} + 6'd10;\n\n  wire guard    = prod_e[idx_g];               // first bit shifted out\n  wire sticky   = below_x[S - 5'd2];           // any bit below the guard\n  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);\n\n  // 8. rounding carry-out straight from prod (no shifter/incrementer in this path):\n  //    normal: carry <=> bits prod[S+10:S] all ones & guard\n  //    subnormal: carry <=> bits prod[S+9:S] all ones & guard\n  wire win9   = bn_e[idx_w9]  & ~bn_e[idx_g];\n  wire win10  = bn_e[idx_w10] & ~bn_e[idx_g];\n  wire carry  = guard & ((sub & win9) | (~sub & win10));\n\n  wire [9:0]  frac_r     = mant_pre[9:0] + {9'd0, (guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]) & (&(mant_pre[9:0])))};\n\n  // 9. final exponent: normal E = d - 14 + carry ; subnormal field = carry\n  wire signed [6:0] e_fin = d - 7'sd14 + {6'd0, carry};\n  wire              ovf   = (e_fin > 7'sd30);   // only reachable on normal path\n  wire [4:0]        exp_n = e_fin[4:0];         // valid when 1..30\n\n  // 10. pack\n  wire is_nan   = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf   = ~nan_in & (inf_a | inf_b | ovf);\n  wire is_sub   = ~nan_in & ~is_inf & sub;\n  wire zero_in  = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sub_zero = is_sub & ~carry & ~|frac_r;\n  wire is_zero  = ~nan_in & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 :\n                      is_sub ? {4'd0, carry} : exp_n;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : frac_r;\n  wire       sign_y = ~is_nan & s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v3.sv')

[Step 25/60]
// fp_mul_e5f10 v3: unified datapath + carry bypass.
// Key changes vs v2:
//  - balanced (log-depth) leading-zero count trees
//  - rounding carry-out computed directly from prod via prefix-OR arrays
//    (no shifter / incrementer on the exponent critical path)
module fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);
  input  [15:0] a;
  input  [15:0] b;
  output [15:0] y;

  // 1. unpack + classify
  wire        s_a = a[15];
  wire        s_b = b[15];
  wire [4:0]  e_a = a[14:10];
  wire [4:0]  e_b = b[14:10];
  wire [9:0]  f_a = a[9:0];
  wire [9:0]  f_b = b[9:0];

  wire e_a_zero = ~|e_a;
  wire e_b_zero = ~|e_b;
  wire e_a_ones =  &e_a;
  wire e_b_ones =  &e_b;
  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & ~|f_a;
  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & ~|f_b;
  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones &  |f_a;
  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones &  |f_b;
  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & ~|f_a;
  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & ~|f_b;

  wire nan_in = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);
  wire s_y    = s_a ^ s_b;

  // 2. effective significands / exponents (subnormals: no hidden one, eff exp 1)
  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_a_zero, f_a};
  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_b_zero, f_b};
  wire [5:0]  exp_sum = {1'b0, (e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a)} + {1'b0, (e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b)};

  // 3. significand product (22 bits) and balanced leading-zero count
  wire [21:0] prod = m_a * m_b;

  wire g_hi = |prod[21:16];
  wire g_md = |prod[15:8];

  wire h01 = |prod[21:20];
  wire h23 = |prod[19:18];
  wire [2:0] czh0 = prod[21] ? 3'd0 : 3'd1;
  wire [2:0] czh1 = prod[19] ? 3'd2 : 3'd3;
  wire [2:0] czh2 = prod[17] ? 3'd4 : 3'd5;
  wire [2:0] clz_hi = h01 ? czh0 : h23 ? czh1 : czh2;

  wire m01 = |prod[15:12];
  wire [3:0] czm0 = prod[15] ? 4'd0 : prod[14] ? 4'd1 : prod[13] ? 4'd2 : 4'd3;
  wire [3:0] czm1 = prod[11] ? 4'd4 : prod[10] ? 4'd5 : prod[9] ? 4'd6 : 4'd7;
  wire [3:0] clz_md = m01 ? czm0 : czm1;

  wire l01 = |prod[7:4];
  wire [3:0] czl0 = prod[7] ? 4'd0 : prod[6] ? 4'd1 : prod[5] ? 4'd2 : 4'd3;
  wire [3:0] czl1 = prod[3] ? 4'd4 : prod[2] ? 4'd5 : prod[1] ? 4'd6 : prod[0] ? 4'd7 : 4'd8;
  wire [3:0] clz_lo = l01 ? czl0 : czl1;

  wire [4:0] lz = g_hi ? {2'b00, clz_hi} :
                  g_md ? (5'd6 + {1'b0, clz_md}) :
                         (5'd14 - {1'b0, clz_lo});

  // 4. prefix reduction: below[k] = |prod[k:0] (sticky)
  wire [21:0] below;
  genvar k;
  generate
    for (k = 0; k < 22; k = k + 1) begin : g_red
      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];
    end
  endgenerate

  // 5. exponent bookkeeping (signed):
  //    E_raw = exp_sum + p - 35 = d - 14,  d = exp_sum - lz
  //    subnormal result  <=>  E_raw <= 0  <=>  d <= 14
  wire signed [6:0] d    = $signed({1'b0, exp_sum}) - $signed({2'b0, lz});
  wire              sub  = (d <= 7'sd14);

  // 6. shift amount: S = max(11 - lz, 26 - exp_sum, 0)
  wire signed [6:0] s_n  = 7'sd11 - $signed({2'b00, lz});
  wire signed [6:0] s_s  = 7'sd26 - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});
  wire signed [6:0] s_m  = (s_n > s_s) ? s_n : s_s;
  wire [4:0]        S    = s_m[6] ? 5'd0 : s_m[4:0];

  // 7. shift + round (single rounding stage, ties-to-even)
  wire [21:0] shifted   = prod >> S;
  wire [10:0] mant_pre  = shifted[10:0];

  wire [63:0] prod_e = {42'd0, prod};
  wire [31:0] below_x = {10'd0, below};
  wire [4:0]  idx_g   = S - 5'd1;

  wire guard    = prod_e[idx_g];               // first bit shifted out
  wire sticky   = below_x[S - 5'd2];           // any bit below the guard
  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);

  // 8. rounding carry-out without the incrementer:
  //    carry <=> guard & mantissa window all ones (guard is ANDed in anyway)
  wire carry  = guard & (&mant_pre[9:0]) & (sub | mant_pre[10]);

  wire [9:0]  frac_r     = mant_pre[9:0] + {9'd0, round_up};

  // 9. final exponent: normal E = d - 14 + carry ; subnormal field = carry
  wire signed [6:0] e_fin = d - 7'sd14 + {6'd0, carry};
  wire              ovf   = (e_fin > 7'sd30);   // only reachable on normal path
  wire [4:0]        exp_n = e_fin[4:0];         // valid when 1..30

  // 10. pack
  wire is_nan   = nan_in;
  wire is_inf   = ~nan_in & (inf_a | inf_b | ovf);
  wire is_sub   = ~nan_in & ~is_inf & sub;
  wire zero_in  = zero_a | zero_b;
  wire sub_zero = is_sub & ~carry & ~|frac_r;
  wire is_zero  = ~nan_in & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_zero);

  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 :
                      is_sub ? {4'd0, carry} : exp_n;
  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : frac_r;
  wire       sign_y = ~is_nan & s_y;

  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};
endmodule

[ASSISTANT]
v4 (split multiplier) is worse — 137534. Discard. Now building **v5**: dual parallel shift/round paths (normal path with shallow 4-bit shift from lz; subnormal path driven only by exp_sum — no LZC on its control path), merged after rounding with the carry-free selection `sub = (d<=14)`:
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v5: dual parallel shift/round paths, refined.\n//  - normal path: 4-bit shift sr = 11-lz (lz-driven control)\n//  - subnormal path: 5-bit shift ts = 26-exp_sum (exp-driven control, no lz)\n//  - paths merge after rounding; selection sub = (E_raw <= 0) is carry-free\n//  - balanced LZC, carry bypass (guard & window-all-ones), shared sticky tree\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // 1. unpack + classify\n  wire        s_a = a[15];\n  wire        s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0]  e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0]  f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = ~|e_a;\n  wire e_b_zero = ~|e_b;\n  wire e_a_ones =  &e_a;\n  wire e_b_ones =  &e_b;\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & ~|f_a;\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & ~|f_b;\n  wire nan_a  = e_a_ones &  |f_a;\n  wire nan_b  = e_b_ones &  |f_b;\n  wire inf_a  = e_a_ones & ~|f_a;\n  wire inf_b  = e_b_ones & ~|f_b;\n\n  wire nan_in = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire s_y    = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  // 2. effective significands / exponents (subnormals: no hidden one, eff exp 1)\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [5:0]  exp_sum = {1'b0, (e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a)} + {1'b0, (e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b)};\n\n  // 3. significand product (22 bits) and balanced leading-zero count\n  wire [21:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  wire g_hi = |prod[21:16];\n  wire g_md = |prod[15:8];\n\n  wire h01 = |prod[21:20];\n  wire h23 = |prod[19:18];\n  wire [2:0] czh0 = prod[21] ? 4'd0 : prod[6] ? 4'd1 : prod[5] ? 4'd2 : 4'd3;\n  wire [3:0] czl1 = prod[3] ? 4'd4 : prod[2] ? 4'd5 : prod[1] ? 4'd6 : prod[0] ? 4'd7 : 4'd8;\n  wire [3:0] clz_lo = l01 ? czl0 : czl1;\n\n  wire [4:0] lz = g_hi ? {2'b00, clz_hi} :\n                  g_md ? (5'd6 + {1'b0, clz_md}) :\n                         (5'd14 - {1'b0, clz_lo});\n\n  // 4. prefix reduction: below[k] = |prod[k:0] (shared sticky tree)\n  wire [21:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 22; k = k + 1) begin : g_red\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [63:0] prod_e  = {42'd0, prod};\n  wire [31:0] below_x = {10'd0, below};\n\n  // 5. regime selection (carry-free):  E_raw = d - 14 ; sub <=> E_raw <= 0\n  wire signed [6:0] d    = $signed({1'b0, exp_sum}) - $signed({2'b0, lz});\n  wire              sub  = (d <= 7'sd14);\n\n  // 6. normal path: sr = 11 - lz (valid when lz <= 11)\n  wire       need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);\n  wire [4:0] sr         = 5'd11 - lz;\n  wire [21:0] shifted_n = need_right ? (5'd6 + {1'b0, clz_md}) :\n                         (5'd14 - {1'b0, clz_lo});\n\n  // 4. prefix reduction: below[k] = |prod[k:0] (shared sticky tree)\n  wire [21:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 22; k = k + 1) begin : g_red\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [63:0] prod_e  = {42'd0, prod};\n  wire [31:0] below_x = {10'd0, below};\n\n  // 5. regime selection (carry-free):  E_raw = d - 14 ; sub <=> E_raw <= 0\n  wire signed [6:0] d    = $signed({1'b0, exp_sum}) - $signed({2'b0, lz});\n  wire              sub  = (d <= 7'sd14);\n\n  // 6. normal path: sr = 11 - lz (lz <= 11 whenever ~sub for valid inputs)\n  wire [4:0]  sr         = 5'd11 - lz;\n  wire [21:0] shifted_n  = prod >> sr;\n  wire [10:0] mant_n     = shifted_n[10:0];\n  wire guard_n  = prod_e[sr - 5'd1];\n  wire sticky_n = below_x[sr - 5'd2];\n  wire round_n  = guard_n & (sticky_n | mant_n[0]);\n  wire [9:0] frac_n  = mant_n[9:0] + {9'd0, round_n};\n  wire       carry_n = guard_n & (&mant_n[10:0]);\n\n  // 7. subnormal path: ts = 26 - exp_sum\n  wire [4:0]  ts         = 6'd26 - exp_sum;\n  wire [21:0] shifted_s  = prod >> ts;\n  wire [10:0] mant_s     = shifted_s[10:0];\n  wire guard_s  = prod_e[ts - 5'd1];\n  wire sticky_s = below_x[ts - 5'd2];\n  wire round_s  = guard_s & (sticky_s | mant_s[0]);\n  wire [9:0] frac_s  = mant_s[9:0] + {9'd0, round_s};\n  wire       carry_s = guard_s & (&mant_s[9:0]);\n\n  // 8. final exponent (normal path): E = d - 14 + carry_n\n  wire signed [6:0] e_norm = d - 7'sd14 + {6'd0, carry_n};\n  wire              ovf    = (e_norm > 7'sd30);\n\n  // 9. pack\n  wire is_nan   = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf   = ~nan_in & (inf_a | inf_b | ovf);\n  wire is_sub   = ~nan_in & ~is_inf & sub;\n  wire zero_in  = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sub_zero = is_sub & ~carry_s & ~|frac_s;\n  wire is_zero  = ~nan_in & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 :\n                      is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : e_norm[4:0];\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 :\n                      sub ? frac_s : frac_n;\n  wire       sign_y = ~is_nan & s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v7.sv')

sub <=> E_raw <= 0\n  wire signed [6:0] d    = $signed({1'b0, exp_sum}) - $signed({2'b0, lz});\n  wire              sub  = (d <= 7'sd14);\n\n  // 6. normal path: sr = 11 - lz (lz <= 11 whenever ~sub for valid inputs)\n  wire [4:0]  sr         = 5'd11 - lz;\n  wire [21:0] shifted_n  = prod >> sr;\n  wire [10:0] mant_n     = shifted_n[10:0];\n  wire [4:0]  idx_gn     = sr - 5'd1;\n  wire [4:0]  idx_sn     = sr - 5'd2;\n  wire guard_n  = prod_e[idx_gn];\n  wire sticky_n = below_x[idx_sn];\n  wire round_n  = guard_n & (sticky_n | mant_n[0]);\n  wire [9:0] frac_n  = mant_n[9:0] + {9'd0, round_n};\n  wire       carry_n = guard_n & (&mant_n[10:0]);\n\n  // 7. subnormal path: ts = 26 - exp_sum\n  wire [4:0]  ts         = 6'd26 - exp_sum;\n  wire [21:0] shifted_s  = prod >> ts;\n  wire [10:0] mant_s     = shifted_s[10:0];\n  wire [4:0]  idx_gs     = ts - 5'd1;\n  wire [4:0]  idx_ss     = ts - 5'd2;\n  wire guard_s  = prod_e[idx_gs];\n  wire sticky_s = below_x[idx_ss];\n  wire round_s  = guard_s & (sticky_s | mant_s[0]);\n  wire [9:0] frac_s  = mant_s[9:0] + {9'd0, round_s};\n  wire       carry_s = guard_s & (&mant_s[9:0]);\n\n  // 8. final exponent (normal path): E = d - 14 + carry_n\n  wire signed [6:0] e_norm = d - 7'sd14 + {6'd0, carry_n};\n  wire              ovf    = (e_norm > 7'sd30);\n\n  // 9. pack\n  wire is_nan   = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf   = ~nan_in & (inf_a | inf_b | ovf);\n  wire is_sub   = ~nan_in & ~is_inf & sub;\n  wire zero_in  = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sub_zero = is_sub & ~carry_s & ~|frac_s;\n  wire is_zero  = ~nan_in & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 :\n                      is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : e_norm[4:0];\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 :\n                      sub ? frac_s : frac_n;\n  wire       sign_y = ~is_nan & s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v8.sv')

(5'd6 + {1'b0, clz_md}) :\n                         (5'd14 - {1'b0, clz_lo});\n\n  // 4. prefix reduction: below[k] = |prod[k:0] (shared sticky tree)\n  wire [21:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 22; k = k + 1) begin : g_red\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [63:0] prod_e  = {42'd0, prod};\n  wire [31:0] below_x = {10'd0, below};\n\n  // 5. regime selection (carry-free):  E_raw = d - 14 ; sub <=> E_raw <= 0\n  wire signed [6:0] d    = $signed({1'b0, exp_sum}) - $signed({2'b0, lz});\n  wire              sub  = (d <= 7'sd14);\n\n  // 6. normal path: sr = 11 - lz (lz <= 11 whenever ~sub for valid inputs)\n  wire [4:0]  sr         = 5'd11 - lz;\n  wire [21:0] shifted_n  = prod >> sr;\n  wire [10:0] mant_n     = shifted_n[10:0];\n  wire guard_n  = prod_e[sr - 5'd1];\n  wire sticky_n = below_x[sr - 5'd2];\n  wire round_n  = guard_n & (sticky_n | mant_n[0]);\n  wire [9:0] frac_n  = mant_n[9:0] + {9'd0, round_n};\n  wire       carry_n = guard_n & (&mant_n[10:0]);\n\n  // 7. subnormal path: ts = 26 - exp_sum\n  wire [4:0]  ts         = 6'd26 - exp_sum;\n  wire [21:0] shifted_s  = prod >> ts;\n  wire [10:0] mant_s     = shifted_s[10:0];\n  wire guard_s  = prod_e[ts - 5'd1];\n  wire sticky_s = below_x[ts - 5'd2];\n  wire round_s  = guard_s & (sticky_s | mant_s[0]);\n  wire [9:0] frac_s  = mant_s[9:0] + {9'd0, round_s};\n  wire       carry_s = guard_s & (&mant_s[9:0]);\n\n  // 8. final exponent (normal path): E = (d + carry_n) - 14\n  wire signed [6:0] e_norm = (d + {6'd0, carry_n}) - 7'sd14;\n  wire              ovf    = (e_norm > 7'sd30);\n\n  // 9. pack\n  wire is_nan   = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf   = ~nan_in & (inf_a | inf_b | ovf);\n  wire is_sub   = ~nan_in & ~is_inf & sub;\n  wire zero_in  = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sub_zero = is_sub & ~carry_s & ~|frac_s;\n  wire is_zero  = ~nan_in & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 :\n                      is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : e_norm[4:0];\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 :\n                      sub ? frac_s : frac_n;\n  wire       sign_y = ~is_nan & s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v10.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
target_delay is fully saturated. One more structural improvement: exp bits [4:1] in the subnormal case are always 0 (only bit 0 = carry_s), so `carry_s` can be removed from bits [4:1] of the exponent path — cutting a whole branch off the exp output mux:
  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v11: v9 + exp pack restructure (carry_s off bits [4:1])\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // 1. unpack + classify\n  wire        s_a = a[15];\n  wire        s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0]  e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0]  f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = ~|e_a;\n  wire e_b_zero = ~|e_b;\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & ~|f_a;\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & ~|f_b;\n  wire nan_a  = (e_a == 5'd31) &  |f_a;\n  wire nan_b  = (e_b == 5'd31) &  |f_b;\n  wire inf_a  = (e_a == 5'd31) & ~|f_a;\n  wire inf_b  = (e_b == 5'd31) & ~|f_b;\n\n  wire nan_in = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire s_y    = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  // 2. effective significands / exponents (subnormals: no hidden one, eff exp 1)\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [5:0]  exp_sum = {1'b0, e_a} + {1'b0, e_b} + {5'd0, e_a_zero} + {5'd0, e_b_zero};\n\n  // 3. significand product (22 bits) and balanced leading-zero count\n  wire [21:0] prod = m_b * m_a;\n\n  wire g_hi = |prod[21:16];\n  wire g_md = |prod[15:8];\n\n  wire h01 = |prod[21:20];\n  wire h23 = |prod[19:18];\n  wire [2:0] czh0 = prod[21] ? 3'd0 : 3'd1;\n  wire [2:0] czh1 = prod[19] ? 3'd2 : 3'd3;\n  wire [2:0] czh2 = prod[17] ? 3'd4 : 3'd5;\n  wire [2:0] clz_hi = h01 ? czh0 : h23 ? czh1 : czh2;\n\n  wire m01 = |prod[15:12];\n  wire [3:0] czm0 = prod[15] ? 4'd0 : prod[14] ? 4'd1 : prod[13] ? 4'd2 : 4'd3;\n  wire [3:0] czm1 = prod[11] ? 4'd4 : prod[10] ? 4'd5 : prod[9] ? 4'd6 : 4'd7;\n  wire [3:0] clz_md = m01 ? czm0 : czm1;\n\n  wire l01 = |prod[7:4];\n  wire [3:0] czl0 = prod[7] ? 4'd0 : prod[6] ? 4'd1 : prod[5] ? 4'd2 : 4'd3;\n  wire [3:0] czl1 = prod[3] ? 4'd4 : prod[2] ? 4'd5 : prod[1] ? 4'd6 : prod[0] ? 4'd7 : 4'd8;\n  wire [3:0] clz_lo = l01 ? czl0 : czl1;\n\n  wire [4:0] lz = g_hi ? {2'b00, clz_hi} :\n                  g_md ? (5'd6 + {1'b0, clz_md}) :\n                         (5'd14 - {1'b0, clz_lo});\n\n  // 4. prefix reduction: below[k] = |prod[k:0] (shared sticky tree)\n  wire [21:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 22; k = k + 1) begin : g_red\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [63:0] prod_e  = {42'd0, prod};\n  wire [31:0] below_x = {10'd0, below};\n\n  // 5. regime selection (carry-free):  E_raw = d - 14 ; sub <=> E_raw <= 0\n  wire signed [6:0] d    = $signed({1'b0, exp_sum}) - $signed({2'b0, lz});\n  wire              sub  = (d <= 7'sd14);\n\n  // 6. normal path: sr = 11 - lz (lz <= 11 whenever ~sub for valid inputs)\n  wire [4:0]  sr         = 5'd11 - lz;\n  wire [21:0] shifted_n  = prod >> sr;\n  wire [10:0] mant_n     = shifted_n[10:0];\n  wire guard_n  = prod_e[sr - 5'd1];\n  wire sticky_n = below_x[sr - 5'd2];\n  wire round_n  = guard_n & (sticky_n | mant_n[0]);\n  wire [9:0] frac_n  = mant_n[9:0] + {9'd0, round_n};\n  wire       carry_n = guard_n & (&mant_n[10:0]);\n\n  // 7. subnormal path: ts = 26 - exp_sum\n  wire [4:0]  ts         = 6'd26 - exp_sum;\n  wire [21:0] shifted_s  = prod >> ts;\n  wire [10:0] mant_s     = shifted_s[10:0];\n  wire guard_s  = prod_e[ts - 5'd1];\n  wire sticky_s = below_x[ts - 5'd2];\n  wire round_s  = guard_s & (sticky_s | mant_s[0]);\n  wire [9:0] frac_s  = mant_s[9:0] + {9'd0, round_s};\n  wire       carry_s = guard_s & (&mant_s[9:0]);\n\n  // 8. final exponent (normal path): E = d - 14 + carry_n\n  wire signed [6:0] e_norm = d - 7'sd14 + {6'd0, carry_n};\n  wire              ovf    = (e_norm > 7'sd30);\n\n  // 9. pack\n  wire is_nan   = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf   = ~nan_in & (inf_a | inf_b | ovf);\n  wire is_sub   = ~nan_in & ~is_inf & sub;\n  wire zero_in  = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sub_zero = is_sub & ~carry_s & ~|frac_s;\n  wire is_zero  = ~nan_in & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_zero);\n  wire is_ni    = is_nan | is_inf;\n\n  // exp[4:1]: 31 for nan/inf, 0 for zero/subnormal, e_norm otherwise\n  // exp[0]  : also 1 for nan/inf, carry_s for subnormal\n  wire [3:0] exp_hi = {4{is_ni}} | ({4{~(is_ni | is_zero | is_sub)}} & e_norm[4:1]);\n  wire       exp_lo = is_ni | ((~is_ni & ~is_zero) & (is_sub ? carry_s : e_norm[0]));\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = {exp_hi, exp_lo};\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 :\n                      sub ? frac_s : frac_n;\n  wire       sign_y = ~is_nan & s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v11.sv')

## Summary of the work

**Starting point** (area 108.7 × delay 1338 = 145,429): correct but with duplicated logic — two full rounding paths (normal + subnormal), a left-shift branch, and serial LZC/limit chains.

**Optimizations applied, in order of impact:**

1. **Unified rounding math + dual parallel paths (v2–v6, 145k → 123k)** — established that results land on either the normal grid (`sr = 11−lz`) or the subnormal grid (`ts = 26−exp_sum`), and the regime select is **carry-free**: `sub ⟺ exp_sum − lz ≤ 14`. The two paths run in parallel and merge after rounding, so each path's shifter has a simple, shallow control. Removing `need_right` (lz>11 only occurs in the dead path) trimmed more.

2. **Rounding-carry bypass (v3 insight)** — `carry = guard & (mantissa window all ones)`, computed straight from the shifter output instead of a carry-propagate through the incrementer, taking the incrementer off the exponent path.

3. **Balanced log-depth LZC trees** replacing 8-deep priority chains, and a shared sticky prefix-OR tree.

4. **Mapping-sensitive expression forms (v7/v9, 123k → 113.5k)** — the tool is highly sensitive to structure: swapping the multiply operand order (`m_b * m_a`, −51 ps) and writing `exp_sum` as a single 4-term sum instead of nested muxes+add (−46 ps) each produced real, reproducible gains.

**Rejected along the way (documented in `design_v*.sv`):** split hi/lo multiplier (137.5k), unified single-shifter datapath at various form tweaks (118–128k), window-all-ones via prefix-OR (incorrect identity — fixed), pack-mux reorder, compare-form `sub`, alternative LZC groupings.

**Verification:** every variant was evaluated against the golden reference over ~18.9M operand pairs including subnormal boundaries, ties-to-even rounding corners, NaN/inf/zero special cases — the final design passes 0/18,874,371 errors.

  -> create_file(content="// fp_mul_e5f10 v17: best (v9/v13) + 11-bit rounding increment form\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  // 1. unpack + classify\n  wire        s_a = a[15];\n  wire        s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0]  e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0]  e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0]  f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0]  f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = ~|e_a;\n  wire e_b_zero = ~|e_b;\n  wire zero_a = e_a_zero & ~|f_a;\n  wire zero_b = e_b_zero & ~|f_b;\n  wire nan_a  = (e_a == 5'd31) &  |f_a;\n  wire nan_b  = (e_b == 5'd31) &  |f_b;\n  wire inf_a  = (e_a == 5'd31) & ~|f_a;\n  wire inf_b  = (e_b == 5'd31) & ~|f_b;\n\n  wire nan_in = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire s_y    = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  // 2. effective significands / exponents (subnormals: no hidden one, eff exp 1)\n  wire [10:0] m_a = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [5:0]  exp_sum = {1'b0, e_a} + {1'b0, e_b} + {5'd0, e_a_zero} + {5'd0, e_b_zero};\n\n  // 3. significand product (22 bits) and balanced leading-zero count\n  wire [21:0] prod = m_b * m_a;\n\n  wire g_hi = |prod[21:16];\n  wire g_md = |prod[15:8];\n\n  wire h01 = |prod[21:20];\n  wire h23 = |prod[19:18];\n  wire [2:0] czh0 = prod[21] ? 3'd0 : 3'd1;\n  wire [2:0] czh1 = prod[19] ? 3'd2 : 3'd3;\n  wire [2:0] czh2 = prod[17] ? 3'd4 : 3'd5;\n  wire [2:0] clz_hi = h01 ? czh0 : h23 ? czh1 : czh2;\n\n  wire m01 = |prod[15:12];\n  wire [3:0] czm0 = prod[15] ? 4'd0 : prod[14] ? 4'd1 : prod[13] ? 4'd2 : 4'd3;\n  wire [3:0] czm1 = prod[11] ? 4'd4 : prod[10] ? 4'd5 : prod[9] ? 4'd6 : 4'd7;\n  wire [3:0] clz_md = m01 ? czm0 : czm1;\n\n  wire l01 = |prod[7:4];\n  wire [3:0] czl0 = prod[7] ? 4'd0 : prod[6] ? 4'd1 : prod[5] ? 4'd2 : 4'd3;\n  wire [3:0] czl1 = prod[3] ? 4'd4 : prod[2] ? 4'd5 : prod[1] ? 4'd6 : prod[0] ? 4'd7 : 4'd8;\n  wire [3:0] clz_lo = l01 ? czl0 : czl1;\n\n  wire [4:0] lz = g_hi ? {2'b00, clz_hi} :\n                  g_md ? (5'd6 + {1'b0, clz_md}) :\n                         (5'd14 - {1'b0, clz_lo});\n\n  // 4. prefix reduction: below[k] = |prod[k:0] (shared sticky tree)\n  wire [21:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < 22; k = k + 1) begin : g_red\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [63:0] prod_e  = {42'd0, prod};\n  wire [31:0] below_x = {10'd0, below};\n\n  // 5. regime selection (carry-free):  E_raw = d - 14 ; sub <=> E_raw <= 0\n  wire signed [6:0] d    = $signed({1'b0, exp_sum}) - $signed({2'b0, lz});\n  wire              sub  = (d <= 7'sd14);\n\n  // 6. normal path: sr = 11 - lz (lz <= 11 whenever ~sub for valid inputs)\n  wire [4:0]  sr         = 5'd11 - lz;\n  wire [21:0] shifted_n  = prod >> sr;\n  wire [10:0] mant_n     = shifted_n[10:0];\n  wire guard_n  = prod_e[sr - 5'd1];\n  wire sticky_n = below_x[sr - 5'd2];\n  wire round_n  = guard_n & (sticky_n | mant_n[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frn        = {1'b0, mant_n[9:0]} + {10'd0, round_n};\n  wire [9:0]  frac_n     = frn[9:0];\n  wire        carry_n    = guard_n & (&mant_n[10:0]);\n\n  // 7. subnormal path: ts = 26 - exp_sum\n  wire [4:0]  ts         = 6'd26 - exp_sum;\n  wire [21:0] shifted_s  = prod >> ts;\n  wire [10:0] mant_s     = shifted_s[10:0];\n  wire guard_s  = prod_e[ts - 5'd1];\n  wire sticky_s = below_x[ts - 5'd2];\n  wire round_s  = guard_s & (sticky_s | mant_s[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frs        = {1'b0, mant_s[9:0]} + {10'd0, round_s};\n  wire [9:0]  frac_s     = frs[9:0];\n  wire        carry_s    = guard_s & (&mant_s[9:0]);\n\n  // 8. final exponent (normal path): E = d - 14 + carry_n\n  wire signed [6:0] e_norm = d - 7'sd14 + {6'd0, carry_n};\n  wire              ovf    = (e_norm > 7'sd30);\n\n  // 9. pack\n  wire is_nan   = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf   = ~nan_in & (inf_a | inf_b | ovf);\n  wire is_sub   = ~nan_in & ~is_inf & sub;\n  wire zero_in  = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire sub_zero = is_sub & ~carry_s & ~|frac_s;\n  wire is_zero  = ~nan_in & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 :\n                      is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : e_norm[4:0];\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 :\n                      sub ? frac_s : frac_n;\n  wire       sign_y = ~is_nan & s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v17.sv')

## Final result

**Best design (design.sv):** area_delay_product = **113,514.82** — 100% correct (0/18,874,371 failures), area 104.01 µm², delay 1091.34 ps.

| Design | Area | Delay | ADP | vs baseline |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| starting_point (v1) | 108.7 | 1338 | 145,429 | — |
| unified datapath (v2/v3) | 90–95 | 1366–1377 | 124–128k | −13% |
| dual parallel paths (v6) | 103.2 | 1189 | 122,670 | −16% |
| + multiply swap (v7) | 104.2 | 1138 | 118,594 | −18% |
| + 4-term exp_sum (v9 = **final**) | **104.0** | **1091** | **113,515** | **−21.9%** |

**Key techniques that produced the win:**
1. **Dual parallel shift/round paths** (normal: `sr = 11−lz`; subnormal: `ts = 26−exp_sum`) merged after rounding with a **carry-free regime select** (`sub ⟺ exp_sum − lz ≤ 14`) — each path gets a simple, shallow shifter control.
2. **Rounding-carry bypass**: `carry = guard & (window all ones)` computed from the shifter output, removing the incrementer from the exponent path.
3. **Balanced log-depth LZC** and a shared sticky prefix-OR tree; dead `need_right` logic removed (lz>11 only occurs in the dead normal path).
4. **Structure-sensitive mapping**: multiply operand order and the 4-term exponent sum changed ABC's mapping by −51 ps and −46 ps respectively — verified reproducible across multiple evaluations.

## Summary

**Final: 113,515 area·delay (area 104.0, delay 1091 ps), 0/18.87M errors** — 21.9% better than the starting point (145,429). Best file: `design.sv` (= `design_v9.sv` = `design_v13.sv` = `design_v17.sv`, all identical mappings).

### 1. Approaches tried
- **v1** (`design.sv` initial): starting point, dual rounding paths with duplicated shifters/adders. Baseline.
- **v2/v3** (`design_v2/3.sv`): unified single-shift datapath (`S = max(11−lz, 26−exp_sum, 0)`), one shifter/round/adder. Smaller (90 µm²) but *slower* (1377 ps).
- **v5/v6** (`design_v5/6.sv`): dual **parallel** shift/round paths (normal: `sr=11−lz`; subnormal: `ts=26−exp_sum`), merged after rounding. Much faster (1189 ps), area 103.
- **v7–v17**: structural/form refinements on v6. Winner: v9.
- Rejected: split hi/lo multiplier (v4), unified path with various control forms (v10–v12, v14).

### 2. Most impactful optimizations
1. **Carry-free regime select**: `sub ⟺ (exp_sum − lz ≤ 14)`. Both paths agree on the E_raw=0 boundary, so neither path's rounding carry feeds the *selection* — this unlocked the fast dual-path structure (~5% ADP).
2. **Dropping `need_right`** (v6): proved lz>11 only occurs when the normal path is dead (both operands subnormal ⇒ always subnormal result), removing a 22-bit mux + masks for free.
3. **Balanced log-depth LZC** replacing 8-deep ternary priority chains; shared sticky prefix-OR tree.
4. **Mapping-sensitive forms** (v7/v9, biggest single wins, −46 ps each): `prod = m_b * m_a` (operand swap) and `exp_sum` as a flat 4-term sum instead of mux-then-add. These are semantics-identical but changed ABC's mapping substantially — confirmed reproducible (v13/v17 map bit-identically to v9).

### 3. What regressed
- Unified single-shifter control (`max`/clamp forms): −13% area but +14–20% delay → worse ADP.
- Split multiplier into parallel partial products + add (v4): serial CPA in series, delay +120 ps.
- "Window all-ones via prefix-OR subtraction" (v3 first attempt): **functionally wrong** — prefix-ORs can't subtract; fixed by using the shifter output: `carry = guard & (&mant window)`.
- Pack-mux reorder (v14), compare-form `sub` (v12), e_norm add-reorder + wide-NOR zero (v10), pair-structured CLZ (v16): all −3–8% each (pure mapping lottery).
- `target_delay` sweeps: dead knob — mapping was identical at 300/500/800/1000 ps (design never meets timing; mapper is already greedy).

### 4. Lessons
- **Delay dominated the product** (~70% of the path), so cutting serial logic depth beat area trimming almost every time; the small-area unified design lost on ADP.
- ABC/Yosys mapping is **chaotically sensitive to expression forms** — operand order, adder associativity, mux nesting. Cheap semantics-preserving "lotteries" paid off twice (−9% combined) but failed ~6 other times; keep each as a separate file and evaluate.
- Prove edge-case equivalences on paper before synthesizing (e.g., lz>11 ⇒ sub regime; both paths agreeing at E_raw=0) — those proofs enabled the two biggest structural wins.
- Next time: get the dual-path architecture right immediately (it's the sweet spot for this problem), then spend steps early on form-lottery around it, rather than exploring the unified datapath first.
